ParlaMint-PL_2018-04-12-sejm-61-2.u505	Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! I am pleased to present the position of the Poselski Club on the government draft law amending the law on government departments and the law on infrastructure of spatial information after the presentation of the report of committees, prints no. 2392 and 2443. Ladies and gentlemen! This proposal amends two laws: the Law of 4 September 1997 on the departments of government administration and the Law of 4 March 2010 on the infrastructure of spatial information. During the committee's work, no key changes were made to the project, which would lead to a different face. That is why we need to say exactly what we said at first reading about this project. The proposal in this project to amend the Government Administration Departments Act implies the transfer of infrastructure issues of spatial information from the Department: IT to the Department: construction, spatial planning and development and housing. As a result of this change, the Minister of Investment and Development will instead be responsible for the infrastructure of spatial information in Poland, which is the consequence of the Minister's taking over of the investment and development management of the department: construction, planning and spatial development and housing under the Regulation of the President of the Council of Ministers of 10 January 2018 on the detailed scope of the minister's activity. According to the projector, this is intended to equip this minister with a tool that will enable him to obtain the necessary information for investment purposes and allow him to monitor the results of his decisions and changes in the economic space of the country in a real way. In turn, the amendments proposed in the Spatial Information Infrastructure Act assume that the creation and maintenance of the Spatial Information Infrastructure geoportal as a central access point for spatial services and the keeping of publicly available collections and spatial data services covered by the infrastructure and providing them with uniform identifiers will be the responsibility of the main surveyor of the country. It is therefore intended to transfer responsibility in this respect from the Minister for Digitisation to the Minister for Investment and Development, which will only perform strategic tasks related to the coordination of spatial information infrastructure and the execution of operational activities will be entrusted to the main surveyor of the country. Ladies and gentlemen! Let me remind you that not much more than a year ago, the Sejm adopted the Act of 16 November 2016 amending certain laws in connection with the creation of the Ministry of Internal Affairs and Administration, which amended, among others. the Government Departments Act and the Spatial Information Infrastructure Act in such a way that the Authority responsible for Spatial Information Infrastructures has been made Minister for Digitalisation and is entrusted with the creation and maintenance of the Spatial Information Infrastructure Geoportal to the full scope of thematic and territorial infrastructure. You should therefore decide which minister should be responsible for what. The transfer of responsibility from one minister to another every few months certainly does not serve anyone, and it merely shows that, despite more than two years of government, you still have no idea how to rule the country. However, I would like to draw attention to one more point. The contested law gives the impression of a regulation designed solely to sanction Prime Minister Mateusz Morawiecki's decision to take over the department: construction, spatial planning and development and housing by the Minister of Investment and Development and to change the distribution of competences between this minister and the main surveyor of the country. However, the PiS would not be himself if, on the occasion of proceding this seemingly orderly regulation, he did not attempt to smuggle solutions to serve only what he really achieved the championship: removing people who have either become uncomfortable or have to free up a seat for the party nominators. In this context Allow me to draw attention to one of the transitional provisions of the proposed regulation, namely Article. Article 5 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 Point 2, according to which the Minister responsible for construction, planning and spatial planning and housing may, within 3 months of the date of entry into force of this Act (...) amend the composition of the Spatial Information Infrastructure Board in the scope referred to in Article. Article 22 (2) (b) 1 point 10 of the Act as amended by Art. 2 as amended by this Act. In other words, within 3 months of the entry into force of the Act, the Minister for Investment and Development may change the composition of the Council by recalling four representatives of scientific institutions or non-governmental organisations appointed by Minister Anna Streżyńska, including. Chairman of this council, Prof. Radosław Wiśniewski - Dean of the Faculty of Geodesy, Spatial Engineering and Construction of UWM in Olsztyn, appointed for this position in January Ub. The Easter of 1025 was held on April 18th. Despite the lack of a strict chronicle, it was probably that day that Archbishop Gniezno Hippolith decorated Bolesław Chrobre's temple with the royal crown. Since the year 1000 and the famous Gniezno convention, our ruler has enjoyed full sovereignty, and in Poland an independent church province with the first archbishop Gaudenty was developing. However, the gesture given to Bolesław Chrobre by Emperor Otto III of his own diadem was not completed in a formal coronation act. The premature death of the young emperor and the long-standing conflict of Chrobre with the successor of Emperor Henry II blocked this possibility, and the crown already prepared in Rome reached the Hungarian ruler Stefan and is today one of the symbols of the independence of the state of our Hungarian nephews. In the spring of 1025 Bolesław Chrobry took advantage of the fact that in the previous year both Emperor Henry and Pope Benedict VIII died, and on this wave of changes he quickly led to his coronation. It happened less than three months before he died. Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, In honor of our ancestors, who regained independence in 1918, let us call on them to record permanently in the national memory and national ceremonial of this act almost a thousand years ago, the erection act of the building, which is the brightest Republic of Poland.
